Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.01 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 1%); h = 1, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.01 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 1%); h = 2, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.05 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 5%); h = 2, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.001 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 0.1%); h = 2, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.05 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 5%); h = 1, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.001 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 0.1%); h = 1, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.

Per-key false-positive levels at α = 0.001 over the K = 1,000 production keys on the N_eff = N = 10,000 realised texts of each corpus cell (the nominal level is 0.1%); h = 2, the cell's longest length. The 1%, 5%, 50%, 95% and 99% key quantiles, the worst sampled key and the shares carry their crossed key-by-document bootstrap intervals (200 resamples of texts and keys; the worst key's interval is the sampled maximum's under resampling, never a population extreme; gate constraint 12); the SD carries its key-bootstrap interval conditional on the texts.
